# Rust Source Growth Observation

Read-only Source census on2026-10-16 from ripgrep visible repository paths. No Cargo metadata/build/rustc/Native/cache effect ran. Both Bun transpiler and Node compile caches were disabled at process birth. This is a source-file census, not a complete crate compilation closure or runtime cache qualification. Module/include expansion and external dependencies were not inferred. No Source copies or temporary script files were created. Python first stopped before Source reads because tomllib is unavailable; an initial buffered ripgrep attempt reached its1MiB output limit and was discarded. This final observation streams paths with65536entries/16MiB path bytes,16MiB per Source file and512MiB total read bounds, joins the actual read-only child, then writes this report.

Observed 32084 Rust Source files, 225617876 bytes and 3719584 newline characters; 2381 package-bearing Cargo manifests. File size alone does not establish crate compile weight. Largest files are candidates for domain decomposition after their actual compiled closure is known.
